From ai-native-annotator.github.io data/skillparse/resources/propbank/en.json: every roleset of every word of the document that PropBank has.

## transition

### transition-01 (change)

- ARG0: causer of change
- ARG1: thing changing
- ARG2: end state
- ARG3: start state

Example 1 (good exercise): I-1 was able *-1 to transition from one exercise to another very seamlessly.
- ARG0: *-1 -- I
- ARG3: from one exercise
- ARG2: to another
- ARGM-MNR: very seamlessly
- rel: transition

Example 2 (Example with all but arg 0): the transition from a small start-up company to a marketing giant with sales last year of $ 1.79 billion
- ARG1: it
- ARG3: from a small start-up company
- ARG2: to a marketing giant with sales last year of $ 1.79 billion
- rel: transition

Example 3 (When to use arg 1 rather than arg 0): USX 's transition from Big Steel to Big Oil
- ARG1: USX 's
- ARG3: from Big Steel
- ARG2: to Big Oil
- rel: transition

Example 4 (When to use arg 0): The country's transition of it's government from a dictatorship to a democracy
- ARG0: The country's
- ARG1: of it's government
- ARG3: from a dictatorship
- ARG2: to a democracy
- rel: transition

Example 5 (One more arg 1): The presidential transition
- ARG1: The presidential
- rel: transition

## United (found as unite)

- ARGM-PRP: to field single candidates in most precincts
